                                  - text: "- Lucky Hit: Chance to gain 5 Fury when hitting an enemy. Double the amount of Fury gained while Berserking and you deal 15(x) Critical Strike Damage while Berserking."
                              - paragraph [ref=e876]:
                                - text: With the above set up, you gain the
                                - emphasis [ref=e877]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                                - strong [ref=e878]
                                - text: bonus of having your
                                - strong [ref=e879]: Arsenal Selection
                                - text: set to Two-Handed Mace, as well as the
                                - emphasis [ref=e880]: Weapon Expertise Technique
                                - emphasis [ref=e881]
                                - text: bonus for the
                                - strong [ref=e882]: Polearm Technique
                                - text: .

                        - generic [ref=e1036]:
                          - heading "Frequently Asked Questions" [level=2] [ref=e1040]
                          - generic [ref=e1047]:
                            - heading "Build Overview" [level=3] [ref=e1048]
                            - heading "Arsenal Selection" [level=3] [ref=e1049]
                            - paragraph [ref=e1050]:
                              - text: This build will be running on a
                              - strong [ref=e1051]: Dual Wield
                              - text: set up in order to take advantage of
                              - generic [ref=e1056]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                              - text: combined with
                              - generic [ref=e1061]: Shard of Verathiel
                              - text: .
                            - list [ref=e1062]:
                              - listitem [ref=e1063]:
                                - strong [ref=e1064]: "If you would like to learn how to set your weapon selection watch this video:"
                                - link "How to Select Weapons video" [ref=e1065] [cursor=pointer]:
                                  - /url: https://youtu.be/dUuG2CJyAvQ?si=Lz-LY5MK3EehP_CC
                            - heading "Fury Management" [level=3] [ref=e1066]
                            - paragraph [ref=e1067]:
                              - generic [ref=e1072]: Shard of Verathiel
                              - text: will cause
                              - generic [ref=e1077]: Frenzy
                              - text: to cost
                              - strong [ref=e1078]: Fury
                              - text: . That combined with
                              - generic [ref=e1083]: Ramaladni's Magnum Opus
                              - text: can be a strain on your fury. Below is your steps to manage that.
                            - heading "Affixes" [level=4] [ref=e1084]
                            - list [ref=e1085]:
                              - listitem [ref=e1086]:
                                - strong [ref=e1087]: Resource Generation
                                - text: will help increase the amount of
                                - strong [ref=e1088]: Fury
                                - emphasis [ref=e1089]
                                - text: you gain and pairs really well with
                                - generic [ref=e1094]: Rallying Cry
                                - text: . You can
                                - strong [ref=e1095]: Temper
                                - text: this onto your
                                - emphasis [ref=e1096]: Rings
                                - text: and
                                - emphasis [ref=e1097]: Amulet.
                              - listitem [ref=e1098]:
                                - strong [ref=e1099]: Fury Regeneration
                                - text: needs to be added onto the Helmet, Chest piece, Pants and Boots.
                              - listitem [ref=e1100]:
                                - text: Do not spam
                                - generic [ref=e1105]: Frenzy
                                - text: if you are not near monsters if you can't maintain
                                - strong [ref=e1106]: Fury
                                - text: .
                            - heading "Defense (Non-Selig)" [level=4] [ref=e1107]
                            - list [ref=e1108]:
                              - listitem [ref=e1109]:
                                - text: Take all defense nodes inside the
                                - emphasis [ref=e1110]: Paragon
                                - text: board first. All
                                - emphasis [ref=e1111]: Maximum Life
                                - text: ","
                                - emphasis [ref=e1112]: Resistances,
                                - text: and
                                - emphasis [ref=e1113]: Armor
                                - text: nodes.
                              - listitem [ref=e1114]:
                                - text: You can use glyphs like
                                - generic [ref=e1119]: Ire
                                - text: or
                                - generic [ref=e1124]: Territorial
                                - text: to help keep yourself alive.
                              - listitem [ref=e1125]:
                                - text: Make sure all your gear has
                                - emphasis [ref=e1126]: Maximum Life
                                - text: on it and you can then add
                                - emphasis [ref=e1127]: Resistances,
                                - text: and
                                - emphasis [ref=e1128]: Armor
                                - text: as needed.
                            - paragraph [ref=e1129]:
                              - strong [ref=e1130]: "If you want to learn how to set your defenses up, watch this video:"
                              - link "How to avoid getting one-shot video" [ref=e1131] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://youtu.be/5IrPJ-H_uLI?si=yDaUSCn8owx0mDWj
                            - heading "Offense" [level=4] [ref=e1132]
                            - paragraph [ref=e1133]:
                              - strong [ref=e1134]: "If you want to learn the basics about Multipliers watch this video:"
                              - link "Additive vs. Multiplicative Damage video" [ref=e1135] [cursor=pointer]:
                                - /url: https://youtu.be/xkaKBMKqPcs?si=kDEk0cc_up4pfUKj
                              - text: .
